          الرمز المكوّن من 6 أرقام الذي أرسله إليك <strong>@vex_otp_bot</strong>
        </div>
        <div class="form-group">
          <label class="form-label">رمز التحقق (6 أرقام)</label>
          <div class="input-wrap">
            <input type="text" id="otpCode" class="form-input otp-input" placeholder="000000"
              maxlength="6" inputmode="numeric" pattern="[0-9]{6}"
              oninput="this.value=this.value.replace(/\D/g,'')" autocomplete="one-time-code">

<script>
function switchTab(tab){
  document.getElementById('tabAdmin').classList.toggle('active', tab==='admin');
  document.getElementById('tabUser').classList.toggle('active', tab==='user');
  document.getElementById('sectionAdmin').classList.toggle('active', tab==='admin');
  document.getElementById('sectionUser').classList.toggle('active', tab==='user');
}
function showOtpStep2(){
  document.getElementById('otpStep1').style.display='none';
  document.getElementById('otpStep2').classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById('otpCode').focus(),100);
}
function resetOtp(){
  document.getElementById('otpStep1').style.display='block';
  document.getElementById('otpStep2').classList.remove('show');
  document.getElementById('otpCode').value='';
  hideAlert('otpError'); hideAlert('otpSuccess');
}
function showAlert(id, msg){
  const el=document.getElementById(id);
  el.childNodes[1] ? el.childNodes[1].textContent=msg : el.innerHTML='<i class="fas fa-info-circle"></i> '+msg;
  el.classList.add('show');
}
function hideAlert(id){document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('show')}

function submitAdmin(e){
  e.preventDefault();
  const f=document.getElementById('adminForm');
  const spinner=document.getElementById('adminSpinner');
  const btnText=document.getElementById('adminBtnText');
  hideAlert('adminError');
  spinner.style.display='block'; btnText.style.display='none';
  fetch(window.location.href,{method:'POST',body:new FormData(f)})
    .then(r=>{
      if(r.redirected){window.location=r.url;return;}
      return r.text().then(html=>{
        document.open();document.write(html);document.close();
      });
    })
    .catch(()=>{ spinner.style.display='none'; btnText.style.display='flex'; f.submit(); });
}

async function verifyOtp(){
  const code=document.getElementById('otpCode').value.trim();
  if(code.length!==6){showAlert('otpError','أدخل رمزاً مكوناً من 6 أرقام');return;}
  hideAlert('otpError'); hideAlert('otpSuccess');
  const spinner=document.getElementById('otpSpinner');
  const btnText=document.getElementById('otpBtnText');
  spinner.style.display='block'; btnText.style.display='none';
  try{
    const r=await fetch('/api/web/auth-code',{
      method:'POST',
      headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body:JSON.stringify({code})
    });
    const d=await r.json();
    if(d.success){
      showAlert('otpSuccess','✅ تم التحقق — جارٍ التحويل...');
      setTimeout(()=>window.location=d.redirect||'/home', 800);
    } else {
      showAlert('otpError', d.error||'رمز غير صالح');
      spinner.style.display='none'; btnText.style.display='flex';
    }
  } catch(e){
    showAlert('otpError','خطأ في الاتصال — حاول مجدداً');
    spinner.style.display='none'; btnText.style.display='flex';
  }
}

document.getElementById('otpCode').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter')verifyOtp();});

// Floating particles
(function(){
  const container=document.getElementById('particles');
  for(let i=0;i<25;i++){
    const p=document.createElement('div');
    p.className='particle';
    p.style.cssText=`left:${Math.random()*100}%;bottom:-5px;animation-duration:${6+Math.random()*10}s;animation-delay:${Math.random()*10}s;width:${1+Math.random()*2}px;height:${1+Math.random()*2}px;opacity:${0.3+Math.random()*0.4}`;
    container.appendChild(p);
  }
})();
</script>

<script>
function switchTab(tab){
  document.getElementById('tabAdmin').classList.toggle('active', tab==='admin');
  document.getElementById('tabUser').classList.toggle('active', tab==='user');
  document.getElementById('sectionAdmin').classList.toggle('active', tab==='admin');
  document.getElementById('sectionUser').classList.toggle('active', tab==='user');
}
function showOtpStep2(){
  document.getElementById('otpStep1').style.display='none';
  document.getElementById('otpStep2').classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById('otpCode').focus(),100);
}
function resetOtp(){
  document.getElementById('otpStep1').style.display='block';
  document.getElementById('otpStep2').classList.remove('show');
  document.getElementById('otpCode').value='';
  hideAlert('otpError'); hideAlert('otpSuccess');
}
function showAlert(id, msg){
  const el=document.getElementById(id);
  el.childNodes[1] ? el.childNodes[1].textContent=msg : el.innerHTML='<i class="fas fa-info-circle"></i> '+msg;
  el.classList.add('show');
}
function hideAlert(id){document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('show')}

function submitAdmin(e){
  e.preventDefault();
  const f=document.getElementById('adminForm');
  const spinner=document.getElementById('adminSpinner');
  const btnText=document.getElementById('adminBtnText');
  hideAlert('adminError');
  spinner.style.display='block'; btnText.style.display='none';
  fetch(window.location.href,{method:'POST',body:new FormData(f)})
    .then(r=>{
      if(r.redirected){window.location=r.url;return;}
      return r.text().then(html=>{
        document.open();document.write(html);document.close();
      });
    })
    .catch(()=>{ spinner.style.display='none'; btnText.style.display='flex'; f.submit(); });
}

async function verifyOtp(){
  const code=document.getElementById('otpCode').value.trim();
  if(code.length!==6){showAlert('otpError','أدخل رمزاً مكوناً من 6 أرقام');return;}
  hideAlert('otpError'); hideAlert('otpSuccess');
  const spinner=document.getElementById('otpSpinner');
  const btnText=document.getElementById('otpBtnText');
  spinner.style.display='block'; btnText.style.display='none';
  try{
    const r=await fetch('/api/web/auth-code',{
      method:'POST',
      headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body:JSON.stringify({code})
    });
    const d=await r.json();
    if(d.success){
      showAlert('otpSuccess','✅ تم التحقق — جارٍ التحويل...');
      setTimeout(()=>window.location=d.redirect||'/home', 800);
    } else {
      showAlert('otpError', d.error||'رمز غير صالح');
      spinner.style.display='none'; btnText.style.display='flex';
    }
  } catch(e){
    showAlert('otpError','خطأ في الاتصال — حاول مجدداً');
    spinner.style.display='none'; btnText.style.display='flex';
  }
}

document.getElementById('otpCode').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter')verifyOtp();});

(function(){
  const container=document.getElementById('particles');
  for(let i=0;i<25;i++){
    const p=document.createElement('div');
    p.className='particle';
    p.style.cssText=`left:${Math.random()*100}%;bottom:-5px;animation-duration:${6+Math.random()*10}s;animation-delay:${Math.random()*10}s;width:${1+Math.random()*2}px;height:${1+Math.random()*2}px;opacity:${0.3+Math.random()*0.4}`;
    container.appendChild(p);
  }
})();

<script>
function switchTab(tab){
  document.getElementById('tabAdmin').classList.toggle('active', tab==='admin');
  document.getElementById('tabUser').classList.toggle('active', tab==='user');
  document.getElementById('sectionAdmin').classList.toggle('active', tab==='admin');
  document.getElementById('sectionUser').classList.toggle('active', tab==='user');
}
function showOtpStep2(){
  document.getElementById('otpStep1').style.display='none';
  document.getElementById('otpStep2').classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById('otpCode').focus(),100);
}
function resetOtp(){
  document.getElementById('otpStep1').style.display='block';
  document.getElementById('otpStep2').classList.remove('show');
  document.getElementById('otpCode').value='';
  hideAlert('otpError'); hideAlert('otpSuccess');
}
function showAlert(id, msg){
  const el=document.getElementById(id);
  el.childNodes[1] ? el.childNodes[1].textContent=msg : el.innerHTML='<i class="fas fa-info-circle"></i> '+msg;
  el.classList.add('show');
}
function hideAlert(id){document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('show')}

function submitAdmin(e){
  e.preventDefault();
  const f=document.getElementById('adminForm');
  const spinner=document.getElementById('adminSpinner');
  const btnText=document.getElementById('adminBtnText');
  hideAlert('adminError');
  spinner.style.display='block'; btnText.style.display='none';
  fetch(window.location.href,{method:'POST',body:new FormData(f)})
    .then(r=>{
      if(r.redirected){window.location=r.url;return;}
      return r.text().then(html=>{
        document.open();document.write(html);document.close();
      });
    })
    .catch(()=>{ spinner.style.display='none'; btnText.style.display='flex'; f.submit(); });
}

async function verifyOtp(){
  const code=document.getElementById('otpCode').value.trim();
  if(code.length!==6){showAlert('otpError','أدخل رمزاً مكوناً من 6 أرقام');return;}
  hideAlert('otpError'); hideAlert('otpSuccess');
  const spinner=document.getElementById('otpSpinner');
  const btnText=document.getElementById('otpBtnText');
  spinner.style.display='block'; btnText.style.display='none';
  try{
    const r=await fetch('/api/web/auth-code',{
      method:'POST',
      headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body:JSON.stringify({code})
    });
    const d=await r.json();
    if(d.success){
      showAlert('otpSuccess','✅ تم التحقق — جارٍ التحويل...');
      setTimeout(()=>window.location=d.redirect||'/home', 800);
    } else {
      showAlert('otpError', d.error||'رمز غير صالح');
      spinner.style.display='none'; btnText.style.display='flex';
    }
  } catch(e){
    showAlert('otpError','خطأ في الاتصال — حاول مجدداً');
    spinner.style.display='none'; btnText.style.display='flex';
  }
}

document.getElementById('otpCode').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter')verifyOtp();});

// Floating particles
(function(){
  const container=document.getElementById('particles');
  for(let i=0;i<25;i++){
    const p=document.createElement('div');
    p.className='particle';
    p.style.cssText=`left:${Math.random()*100}%;bottom:-5px;animation-duration:${6+Math.random()*10}s;animation-delay:${Math.random()*10}s;width:${1+Math.random()*2}px;height:${1+Math.random()*2}px;opacity:${0.3+Math.random()*0.4}`;
    container.appendChild(p);
  }
})();
</script>

<script>
function switchTab(tab){
  document.getElementById('tabAdmin').classList.toggle('active', tab==='admin');
  document.getElementById('tabUser').classList.toggle('active', tab==='user');
  document.getElementById('sectionAdmin').classList.toggle('active', tab==='admin');
  document.getElementById('sectionUser').classList.toggle('active', tab==='user');
}
function showOtpStep2(){
  document.getElementById('otpStep1').style.display='none';
  document.getElementById('otpStep2').classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById('otpCode').focus(),100);
}
function resetOtp(){
  document.getElementById('otpStep1').style.display='block';
  document.getElementById('otpStep2').classList.remove('show');
  document.getElementById('otpCode').value='';
  hideAlert('otpError'); hideAlert('otpSuccess');
}
function showAlert(id, msg){
  const el=document.getElementById(id);
  el.childNodes[1] ? el.childNodes[1].textContent=msg : el.innerHTML='<i class="fas fa-info-circle"></i> '+msg;
  el.classList.add('show');
}
function hideAlert(id){document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('show')}

function submitAdmin(e){
  e.preventDefault();
  const f=document.getElementById('adminForm');
  const spinner=document.getElementById('adminSpinner');
  const btnText=document.getElementById('adminBtnText');
  hideAlert('adminError');
  spinner.style.display='block'; btnText.style.display='none';
  fetch(window.location.href,{method:'POST',body:new FormData(f)})
    .then(r=>{
      if(r.redirected){window.location=r.url;return;}
      return r.text().then(html=>{
        document.open();document.write(html);document.close();
      });
    })
    .catch(()=>{ spinner.style.display='none'; btnText.style.display='flex'; f.submit(); });
}

async function verifyOtp(){
  const code=document.getElementById('otpCode').value.trim();
  if(code.length!==6){showAlert('otpError','أدخل رمزاً مكوناً من 6 أرقام');return;}
  hideAlert('otpError'); hideAlert('otpSuccess');
  const spinner=document.getElementById('otpSpinner');
  const btnText=document.getElementById('otpBtnText');
  spinner.style.display='block'; btnText.style.display='none';
  try{
    const r=await fetch('/api/web/auth-code',{
      method:'POST',
      headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body:JSON.stringify({code})
    });
    const d=await r.json();
    if(d.success){
      showAlert('otpSuccess','✅ تم التحقق — جارٍ التحويل...');
      setTimeout(()=>window.location=d.redirect||'/home', 800);
    } else {
      showAlert('otpError', d.error||'رمز غير صالح');
      spinner.style.display='none'; btnText.style.display='flex';
    }
  } catch(e){
    showAlert('otpError','خطأ في الاتصال — حاول مجدداً');
    spinner.style.display='none'; btnText.style.display='flex';
  }
}

document.getElementById('otpCode').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter')verifyOtp();});

// Floating particles
(function(){
  const container=document.getElementById('particles');
  for(let i=0;i<25;i++){
    const p=document.createElement('div');
    p.className='particle';
    p.style.cssText=`left:${Math.random()*100}%;bottom:-5px;animation-duration:${6+Math.random()*10}s;animation-delay:${Math.random()*10}s;width:${1+Math.random()*2}px;height:${1+Math.random()*2}px;opacity:${0.3+Math.random()*0.4}`;
    container.appendChild(p);
  }
})();
</script>

<script>
function switchTab(tab){
  document.getElementById('tabAdmin').classList.toggle('active', tab==='admin');
  document.getElementById('tabUser').classList.toggle('active', tab==='user');
  document.getElementById('sectionAdmin').classList.toggle('active', tab==='admin');
  document.getElementById('sectionUser').classList.toggle('active', tab==='user');
}
function showOtpStep2(){
  document.getElementById('otpStep1').style.display='none';
  document.getElementById('otpStep2').classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById('otpCode').focus(),100);
}
function resetOtp(){
  document.getElementById('otpStep1').style.display='block';
  document.getElementById('otpStep2').classList.remove('show');
  document.getElementById('otpCode').value='';
  hideAlert('otpError'); hideAlert('otpSuccess');
}
function showAlert(id, msg){
  const el=document.getElementById(id);
  el.childNodes[1] ? el.childNodes[1].textContent=msg : el.innerHTML='<i class="fas fa-info-circle"></i> '+msg;
  el.classList.add('show');
}
function hideAlert(id){document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('show')}

function submitAdmin(e){
  e.preventDefault();
  const f=document.getElementById('adminForm');
  const spinner=document.getElementById('adminSpinner');
  const btnText=document.getElementById('adminBtnText');
  hideAlert('adminError');
  spinner.style.display='block'; btnText.style.display='none';
  fetch(window.location.href,{method:'POST',body:new FormData(f)})
    .then(r=>{
      if(r.redirected){window.location=r.url;return;}
      return r.text().then(html=>{
        document.open();document.write(html);document.close();
      });
    })
    .catch(()=>{ spinner.style.display='none'; btnText.style.display='flex'; f.submit(); });
}

async function verifyOtp(){
  const code=document.getElementById('otpCode').value.trim();
  if(code.length!==6){showAlert('otpError','أدخل رمزاً مكوناً من 6 أرقام');return;}
  hideAlert('otpError'); hideAlert('otpSuccess');
  const spinner=document.getElementById('otpSpinner');
  const btnText=document.getElementById('otpBtnText');
  spinner.style.display='block'; btnText.style.display='none';
  try{
    const r=await fetch('/api/web/auth-code',{
      method:'POST',
      headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body:JSON.stringify({code})
    });
    const d=await r.json();
    if(d.success){
      showAlert('otpSuccess','✅ تم التحقق — جارٍ التحويل...');
      setTimeout(()=>window.location=d.redirect||'/home', 800);
    } else {
      showAlert('otpError', d.error||'رمز غير صالح');
      spinner.style.display='none'; btnText.style.display='flex';
    }
  } catch(e){
    showAlert('otpError','خطأ في الاتصال — حاول مجدداً');
    spinner.style.display='none'; btnText.style.display='flex';
  }
}

document.getElementById('otpCode').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter')verifyOtp();});

// Floating particles
(function(){
  const container=document.getElementById('particles');
  for(let i=0;i<25;i++){
    const p=document.createElement('div');
    p.className='particle';
    p.style.cssText=`left:${Math.random()*100}%;bottom:-5px;animation-duration:${6+Math.random()*10}s;animation-delay:${Math.random()*10}s;width:${1+Math.random()*2}px;height:${1+Math.random()*2}px;opacity:${0.3+Math.random()*0.4}`;
    container.appendChild(p);
  }
})();
</script>

<script>
function switchTab(tab){
  document.getElementById('tabAdmin').classList.toggle('active', tab==='admin');
  document.getElementById('tabUser').classList.toggle('active', tab==='user');
  document.getElementById('sectionAdmin').classList.toggle('active', tab==='admin');
  document.getElementById('sectionUser').classList.toggle('active', tab==='user');
}
function showOtpStep2(){
  document.getElementById('otpStep1').style.display='none';
  document.getElementById('otpStep2').classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById('otpCode').focus(),100);
}
function resetOtp(){
  document.getElementById('otpStep1').style.display='block';
  document.getElementById('otpStep2').classList.remove('show');
  document.getElementById('otpCode').value='';
  hideAlert('otpError'); hideAlert('otpSuccess');
}
function showAlert(id, msg){
  const el=document.getElementById(id);
  el.childNodes[1] ? el.childNodes[1].textContent=msg : el.innerHTML='<i class="fas fa-info-circle"></i> '+msg;
  el.classList.add('show');
}
function hideAlert(id){document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('show')}

function submitAdmin(e){
  e.preventDefault();
  const f=document.getElementById('adminForm');
  const spinner=document.getElementById('adminSpinner');
  const btnText=document.getElementById('adminBtnText');
  hideAlert('adminError');
  spinner.style.display='block'; btnText.style.display='none';
  fetch(window.location.href,{method:'POST',body:new FormData(f)})
    .then(r=>{
      if(r.redirected){window.location=r.url;return;}
      return r.text().then(html=>{
        document.open();document.write(html);document.close();
      });
    })
    .catch(()=>{ spinner.style.display='none'; btnText.style.display='flex'; f.submit(); });
}

async function verifyOtp(){
  const code=document.getElementById('otpCode').value.trim();
  if(code.length!==6){showAlert('otpError','أدخل رمزاً مكوناً من 6 أرقام');return;}
  hideAlert('otpError'); hideAlert('otpSuccess');
  const spinner=document.getElementById('otpSpinner');
  const btnText=document.getElementById('otpBtnText');
  spinner.style.display='block'; btnText.style.display='none';
  try{
    const r=await fetch('/api/web/auth-code',{
      method:'POST',
      headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body:JSON.stringify({code})
    });
    const d=await r.json();
    if(d.success){
      showAlert('otpSuccess','✅ تم التحقق — جارٍ التحويل...');
      setTimeout(()=>window.location=d.redirect||'/home', 800);
    } else {
      showAlert('otpError', d.error||'رمز غير صالح');
      spinner.style.display='none'; btnText.style.display='flex';
    }
  } catch(e){
    showAlert('otpError','خطأ في الاتصال — حاول مجدداً');
    spinner.style.display='none'; btnText.style.display='flex';
  }
}

document.getElementById('otpCode').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter')verifyOtp();});

// Floating particles
(function(){
  const container=document.getElementById('particles');
  for(let i=0;i<25;i++){
    const p=document.createElement('div');
    p.className='particle';
    p.style.cssText=`left:${Math.random()*100}%;bottom:-5px;animation-duration:${6+Math.random()*10}s;animation-delay:${Math.random()*10}s;width:${1+Math.random()*2}px;height:${1+Math.random()*2}px;opacity:${0.3+Math.random()*0.4}`;
    container.appendChild(p);
  }
})();
</script>

<script>
function switchTab(tab){
  document.getElementById('tabAdmin').classList.toggle('active', tab==='admin');
  document.getElementById('tabUser').classList.toggle('active', tab==='user');
  document.getElementById('sectionAdmin').classList.toggle('active', tab==='admin');
  document.getElementById('sectionUser').classList.toggle('active', tab==='user');
}
function showOtpStep2(){
  document.getElementById('otpStep1').style.display='none';
  document.getElementById('otpStep2').classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById('otpCode').focus(),100);
}
function resetOtp(){
  document.getElementById('otpStep1').style.display='block';
  document.getElementById('otpStep2').classList.remove('show');
  document.getElementById('otpCode').value='';
  hideAlert('otpError'); hideAlert('otpSuccess');
}
function showAlert(id, msg){
  const el=document.getElementById(id);
  el.childNodes[1] ? el.childNodes[1].textContent=msg : el.innerHTML='<i class="fas fa-info-circle"></i> '+msg;
  el.classList.add('show');
}
function hideAlert(id){document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('show')}

function submitAdmin(e){
  e.preventDefault();
  const f=document.getElementById('adminForm');
  const spinner=document.getElementById('adminSpinner');
  const btnText=document.getElementById('adminBtnText');
  hideAlert('adminError');
  spinner.style.display='block'; btnText.style.display='none';
  fetch(window.location.href,{method:'POST',body:new FormData(f)})
    .then(r=>{
      if(r.redirected){window.location=r.url;return;}
      return r.text().then(html=>{
        document.open();document.write(html);document.close();
      });
    })
    .catch(()=>{ spinner.style.display='none'; btnText.style.display='flex'; f.submit(); });
}

async function verifyOtp(){
  const code=document.getElementById('otpCode').value.trim();
  if(code.length!==6){showAlert('otpError','أدخل رمزاً مكوناً من 6 أرقام');return;}
  hideAlert('otpError'); hideAlert('otpSuccess');
  const spinner=document.getElementById('otpSpinner');
  const btnText=document.getElementById('otpBtnText');
  spinner.style.display='block'; btnText.style.display='none';
  try{
    const r=await fetch('/api/web/auth-code',{
      method:'POST',
      headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body:JSON.stringify({code})
    });
    const d=await r.json();
    if(d.success){
      showAlert('otpSuccess','✅ تم التحقق — جارٍ التحويل...');
      setTimeout(()=>window.location=d.redirect||'/home', 800);
    } else {
      showAlert('otpError', d.error||'رمز غير صالح');
      spinner.style.display='none'; btnText.style.display='flex';
    }
  } catch(e){
    showAlert('otpError','خطأ في الاتصال — حاول مجدداً');
    spinner.style.display='none'; btnText.style.display='flex';
  }
}

document.getElementById('otpCode').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter')verifyOtp();});

// Floating particles
(function(){
  const container=document.getElementById('particles');
  for(let i=0;i<25;i++){
    const p=document.createElement('div');
    p.className='particle';
    p.style.cssText=`left:${Math.random()*100}%;bottom:-5px;animation-duration:${6+Math.random()*10}s;animation-delay:${Math.random()*10}s;width:${1+Math.random()*2}px;height:${1+Math.random()*2}px;opacity:${0.3+Math.random()*0.4}`;
    container.appendChild(p);
  }
})();
</script>
